Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe—it adds a nice pop of color and balances out your more oversized staples. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** on top (worn open) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for a classic, comfortable Y2K look.

**Option 2: Trendy & Elevated**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist, layer on the **Vintage black denim jacket**, and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots** for a cool, vintage-inspired vibe.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a fun, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Here are two easy ways to style it using your current pieces:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. The contrast between the fitted, cropped tee and the baggy denim creates a classic Y2K silhouette. Complete the look with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a balanced, everyday outfit.

**Option 2: Trendy & Elevated**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and finish with the **Black combat boots** to give the "cottagecore" butterfly print a bit of an edgy, grunge twist. Throw on the **Vintage black denim jacket** if you need an extra layer.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Y2K Streetwear (The "Cool Girl" Look)**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag

**Option 2: Effortless Chic**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots (for a nice contrast with the soft butterfly print)
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a perfect addition because they offer a more structured, straight-leg fit to balance your current collection of baggy and wide-leg bottoms.

Here are two easy ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* This is a timeless, balanced silhouette that leans into the 90s aesthetic. Tucking in the tank and adding the belt gives the 501s a nice defined waist.

**2. The Streetwear Grunge Look**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The cropped length of the hoodie pairs perfectly with the higher rise of the 501s, while the combat boots lean into the vintage, worn-in vibe of the denim.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored these vintage Levi's 501s and they have the perfect structured fit to balance out my rotation of baggy jeans. I just listed these for $38 on depop, so grab them before I change my mind and keep them for myself!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a perfect addition to your wardrobe because they provide a structured, straight-leg contrast to your existing baggy jeans and wide-leg trousers.

Here are two easy ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
Pair the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** with your **White ribbed tank top** tucked in. Throw on the **Vintage black denim jacket** and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to pull the outfit together.

**2. The Streetwear Edge**
Tuck the **Black cropped zip hoodie** into the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans**. Pair with your **Black combat boots** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a sharp, cohesive look that leans into that vintage streetwear vibe.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These Vintage Levi's 501s are a fantastic investment! Since you already have baggy jeans, these 501s will provide a slightly more structured, classic silhouette that balances out your wardrobe perfectly.

Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The "Off-Duty Cool" Look**
Pair the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** with the **White ribbed tank top** tucked in, topped with the **Vintage black denim jacket**. Finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a clean, timeless streetwear vibe.

**2. The "Edgy Minimalist" Look**
Tuck the front of the **Oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** into the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans**. Cinch the waist with the **Brown leather belt** and pair with the **Black combat boots** to create a great mix of cozy textures and structured denim.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a great find! They’ll offer a slightly more structured, classic silhouette compared to your baggy jeans. Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Tucking the white tank into the 501s and cinching the waist with your brown belt creates a clean, timeless silhouette that balances the denim-on-denim aesthetic.

**2. The Edgy Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The straight-leg cut of the 501s tucks perfectly into your combat boots. The cropped hoodie keeps the outfit looking intentional and streamlined rather than too bulky.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally snagged the perfect vintage Levi's 501s in this ideal medium wash for only $38. I’m obsessed with the structured fit, so head over to my depop if you want to give these a new home!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage Levi’s 501s are a fantastic investment! Since they offer a more structured, classic fit compared to your baggy jeans, they will add versatility to your closet. 

Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Effortless Everyday**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* A classic Americana look that highlights the medium wash of the denim.

**2. The Edgy Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The cropped hoodie balances the straight-leg silhouette of the 501s, creating a sharp, intentional proportion.
```

```
This butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider-leg bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** for a layered look, and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for that authentic Y2K aesthetic.

**Option 2: Minimalist Chic**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Cinch the waist with the **Brown leather belt** and throw on the **Vintage black denim jacket** to tie the look together. Complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots** for a bit of edge.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a fantastic find! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider bottoms perfectly. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The Balanced Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. The contrast between the tight top and loose denim creates a classic Y2K silhouette. Finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a casual, trendy vibe.

**2. The Casual Minimalist Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and wear the **Black combat boots** to give the outfit a bit of an edge. Throw on the **Vintage black denim jacket** if you need an extra layer!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your looser bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Why it works:** The fitted tee contrasts perfectly with the baggy jeans, while the cropped hoodie keeps the silhouette flattering.

**Option 2: The "Cottagecore" Minimalist Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   **Why it works:** The earthy tones of the khakis and brown belt ground the butterfly print, creating a trendy, balanced vintage aesthetic.
```

```
Hi there! That butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your closet—it’s a great way to add some Y2K flair to the pieces you already own. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The "Cool-Girl" Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **baggy straight-leg jeans**. The fitted silhouette of the tee will balance out the volume of the jeans perfectly. Add your **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag** to complete the vibe.

**Option 2: The Soft-Cottagecore Aesthetic**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **brown leather belt** to define your waist, and finish the look with your **black combat boots** for a bit of edge that keeps the outfit from feeling too sweet.
```

```
This butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because the tee is fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Y2K Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a classic, effortless look. Throw on the **Vintage black denim jacket** if you need an extra layer.

**Option 2: Trendy Earth Tones**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to cinch the waist and finish the outfit with your **Black combat boots** to give the "cottagecore" graphic a bit of an edgy, grunge contrast.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since you already have a solid streetwear foundation, here are two ways to style that flannel:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** (worn open) over the **White ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans** and **Black combat boots**. It’s the ultimate effortless, edgy outfit.

**2. The Elevated Relaxed Look**
Tuck the **White ribbed tank top** into the **Wide-leg khaki trousers** and add the **Brown leather belt**. Wear the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** open as a lightweight jacket on top and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers**. Perfect for a stylish, balanced everyday look.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This oversized flannel is a perfect, versatile addition to your closet. Here are two ways to style it using your current pieces:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **Oversized flannel** over the **White ribbed tank top**. Pair this with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans** and the **Black combat boots**. Leave the flannel unbuttoned for an effortless, streetwear-inspired silhouette.

**Option 2: Textured Neutral Layers**
Tuck the **White ribbed tank top** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Layer the **Oversized flannel** over the top, worn open, and finish the look with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Brown leather belt** to pull the earth tones together.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This flannel is a great find! It’s versatile enough to lean into both your grunge and minimal aesthetics. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** (unbuttoned) over your **White ribbed tank top**. Pair it with the **Baggy straight-leg jeans** and your **Black combat boots**. This creates that quintessential 90s streetwear vibe.

**Option 2: Elevated Earth Tones**
Tuck the **White ribbed tank top** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Wear the **Oversized Flannel Shirt** open as a light layer, and finish the look with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Brown leather belt** to tie the colors together.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored this vintage Woolrich oversized flannel for only $22, and it’s honestly the perfect layering piece for fall. I’ve been obsessed with hunting for gems like this on thredUp lately because it fits that grungy-meets-minimal vibe so perfectly!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since the flannel is oversized, it’s perfect for playing with proportions. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The "Grunge Minimalist" Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** (left unbuttoned) over your **white ribbed tank top**. Pair this with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and finish the look with your **black combat boots**. This balances the loose fit of the shirt and jeans with the fitted tank underneath.

**2. The "Streetwear Contrast" Look**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers** and wear the **oversized flannel shirt** open as a lightweight jacket. Add your **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag** for a clean, effortless outfit that plays with earth tones and texture.
```

```
Finally scored this perfectly worn-in Woolrich flannel for just $22. I’m obsessed with how the oversized fit looks layered over a tank, and I honestly can’t stop shopping on thredUp for gems like this.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since the flannel is oversized, it’s perfect for playing with proportions. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge (Edgy & Comfortable)**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Layer:** Oversized Flannel Shirt (worn open)
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag

**Option 2: The Modern Minimalist (Clean & Elevated)**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Layer:** Oversized Flannel Shirt (buttoned up and tucked into the waistband)
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt

**Styling Tip:** Since the flannel is oversized, try doing a "French tuck" (tucking just the front) into the khaki trousers to give yourself a bit more shape while keeping that relaxed vibe!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a fantastic find—it’s versatile, lightweight, and hits that perfect retro streetwear vibe. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**Look 1: The Off-Duty Streetwear Fit**
Layer the **white ribbed tank top** under the track jacket (keep the jacket unzipped). Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and finish with the **chunky white sneakers**. This leans into the 90s athletic aesthetic and feels effortless for a weekend out.

**Look 2: The Elevated Minimalist Mix**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Throw the track jacket on over the top, paired with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. The contrast between the sporty jacket and the structured trousers creates a really cool, modern silhouette.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally snagged this vintage Champion track jacket and the navy and white stripes are giving total 90s nostalgia. I couldn’t believe my luck finding it for only $45 on Poshmark, and it’s already becoming my new go-to for that perfect effortless streetwear look.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! Since the jacket is lightweight and has that classic 90s aesthetic, it will integrate perfectly with your current pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The "Off-Duty" Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (worn underneath the jacket, left unzipped)
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the 90s athletic vibe while keeping the proportions balanced with your baggy denim.

**Option 2: The Modern Minimalist Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the navy track jacket with the khaki trousers gives you a polished, retro-sporty contrast that feels very current.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a fantastic find! Since it’s lightweight and has that classic athletic vibe, it will pair perfectly with the pieces you already own. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Streetwear Classic**
Layer the **90s Track Jacket** over your **white ribbed tank top** and pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans**. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag**. It’s the ultimate effortless 90s aesthetic.

**Option 2: Elevated Athleisure**
Wear the **90s Track Jacket** zipped up with your **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add the **brown leather belt** for a polished detail and pair it with the **chunky white sneakers**. This mix of athletic and earth tones creates a cool, modern silhouette.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This track jacket is a great score! It leans perfectly into the 90s streetwear vibe you already have going. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Off-Duty Streetwear Look**
Pair the **90s Track Jacket** with your **White ribbed tank top** and **Baggy straight-leg jeans**. Finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** and your **Black crossbody bag**. Leave the jacket unzipped to show off the tank for a classic, effortless silhouette.

**Option 2: The Elevated Minimalist Look**
Tuck the **White ribbed tank top** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers** and layer the **90s Track Jacket** over the top. Add the **Brown leather belt** and your **Chunky white sneakers** for a clean, athletic-meets-minimalist aesthetic.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a great versatile find! Here are two effortless ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**Option 1: The "Streetwear Chic" Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket (worn unzipped)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works: This leans into the 90s aesthetic. The fitted tank balances the baggy jeans and the lightweight jacket perfectly.*

**Option 2: The "Elevated Casual" Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket (zipped up)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works: The contrast between the sporty track jacket and the minimal trousers creates a clean, intentional "model-off-duty" vibe.*
```

```
Finally found the perfect 90s Champion track jacket for that sporty-but-chic look I’ve been hunting for. I snagged this navy and white gem for $45 on Poshmark and I’m already obsessed with how effortless it looks with baggy jeans.
```

```
This 90s silk slip dress is a fantastic versatile find! Because it’s lightweight and feminine, it will balance perfectly with your more structured, edgy pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair this with your **black combat boots** for that classic, effortless 90s silhouette.

**2. The "Edgy Layered" Look**
Wear the slip dress as your base and throw your **vintage black denim jacket** over the top. Finish the outfit with your **chunky white sneakers** to give the feminine floral print a cool, modern streetwear edge.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This silk slip dress is a fantastic find—it’s the perfect "base layer" to contrast with your edgier streetwear pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag** for an effortless, cozy-meets-feminine vibe.

**2. The Edgy Street-Style Layer**
Wear the slip dress on its own and throw your **vintage black denim jacket** over the top to balance the delicate fabric with the structured, vintage denim. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers** for a comfortable, cool outfit that’s perfect for brunch or errands.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! A silk slip dress is incredibly versatile because it works just as well as a skirt or as a standalone piece. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**Option 1: The "90s Grunge" Layer (Casual)**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair this with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. The contrast between the delicate silk and the slouchy sweatshirt creates that classic effortless 90s aesthetic.

**Option 2: The "Edgy Feminine" Look (Transitional)**
Wear the slip dress on its own and layer your **vintage black denim jacket** over the top. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers**. This balances the romantic floral print with a cool, streetwear edge, making the dress feel much more wearable for everyday outings.
```

Fit card:

```
Finally scored this dream 90s floral silk slip dress for only $30! I’m obsessed with how versatile it is, and I just posted it to my depop shop if you want to snag it.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a fantastic addition! They offer a more structured, straight-leg silhouette that balances out your existing baggy and wide-leg pieces.

Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
Pair the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** with your **White ribbed tank top** and the **Vintage black denim jacket**. Finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag**. It’s a timeless, effortless outfit perfect for daily errands.

**2. The Streetwear Edge**
Tuck the **Black cropped zip hoodie** into the **Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans** and cinch with your **Brown leather belt**. Pair with the **Black combat boots** for a sharp, balanced silhouette that highlights the waist against the straight-leg denim.
```


Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a perfect addition because they offer a more structured, classic fit compared to your baggy jeans. Here are two ways to style them:

**Outfit 1: The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (tucked in)
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Bottom:** Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* This is a timeless, effortless silhouette that balances the fitted tank with the structured denim and classic footwear.

**Outfit 2: The Streetwear Edge**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Bottom:** Vintage Levi's 501 Jeans
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The cropped length of the hoodie highlights the waist of the 501s, while the boots and crossbody bag lean into that cool, gritty streetwear vibe.
```

```
Finally found the perfect pair of vintage Levi’s 501s that actually hit at the waist and give that structured look I’ve been hunting for. I scored these for just $38 and I'm already obsessed with how they style, so go check out my shop on depop before I change my mind!
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
These vintage 501s are a fantastic investment—they offer a structured, straight-leg silhouette that perfectly bridges the gap between your baggy jeans and wide-leg trousers.

Here are two ways to style them:

**1. The Classic Off-Duty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket 
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* The fitted tank balances the straight-leg cut of the 501s, while the cropped jacket keeps the proportions flattering.

**2. The Elevated Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The cropped hoodie hits right at the waistline of the 501s, creating a sharp, intentional silhouette that pairs perfectly with the edgy boots.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
Hi there! That butterfly baby tee is a perfect find—it’s going to add a great pop of color to your wardrobe. Here are two ways to style it using your current pieces:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. The contrast between the fitted crop top and the baggy denim creates that classic Y2K silhouette. Finish the look with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a balanced, everyday vibe.

**Option 2: Trendy & Elevated**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add your **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and pair with the **Black combat boots** for an edgy, cottagecore-meets-streetwear look. If it's chilly, throw your **Vintage black denim jacket** over the top.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
Here are two ways to style that cute baby tee using your existing wardrobe:

**Option 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **baggy straight-leg jeans**. Because the tee is cropped and fitted, it perfectly balances the volume of the baggy denim. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag** for an effortless, on-trend outfit.

**Option 2: The Minimalist-Cute Contrast**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers**. This creates a nice contrast between the soft, feminine butterfly print and the structured, earthy tones of the pants. Add your **brown leather belt** to pull the colors together, and complete the outfit with your **black combat boots** for a slightly edgy, vintage-inspired vibe.
```

```
**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**

**Option 2: Edgy Contrast**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Layer your **Vintage black denim jacket** on top and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots**. This plays with the contrast between "cottagecore" butterflies and grunge-inspired accessories.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This butterfly baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your looser bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Outfit 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The fitted tee contrasts with the baggy denim for a classic 2000s silhouette, and the black jacket ties in the dark wash denim.

**Outfit 2: The Minimalist Cottagecore Mix**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* The khaki trousers ground the playful butterfly print, creating a polished, earthy aesthetic. The belt helps define your waist against the wider trouser cut.
```

```
Found this perfect butterfly baby tee today and I’m honestly obsessed with the Y2K energy. Snagged it for just $18 over on depop, and it’s going to be my new go-to for balancing out all my baggy jeans!
```

```
This baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out the volume of your existing bottoms beautifully. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** on top (worn open or layered over) and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** for a classic Y2K-inspired look.

**Option 2: Casual Earthy Chic**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Cinch the waist with the **Brown leather belt** and pair it with your **Black combat boots** to ground the softer colors of the shirt with an edgy finish.
```

```
That red and black flannel is a perfect staple for your wardrobe! Since it's oversized, here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** (left open) over your **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and finish the look with your **black combat boots**. The proportions work perfectly for that effortless, street-ready vibe.

**Option 2: Textured Earth Tones**
```

```
This red/black flannel is a fantastic, versatile addition to your closet! Since it's oversized, it’s perfect for layering. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** open over your **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with the **baggy straight-leg jeans** and **black combat boots**. Use the **black crossbody bag** to keep the vibe effortless and edgy.

**Option 2: The Elevated Streetwear Look**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into the **wide-leg khaki trousers** and cinch with the **brown leather belt**. Wear the **oversized flannel shirt** open as a lightweight jacket on top, and finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers**.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a fantastic, versatile addition to your wardrobe! Since you already own some solid staples, here are two ways to style that flannel:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Pair the **oversized flannel** unbuttoned over your **white ribbed tank top**. Tuck the tank into your **baggy straight-leg jeans**, add the **black combat boots**, and use the **black crossbody bag** to tie it all together. The contrast between the fitted tank and the oversized flannel is perfect for that classic streetwear aesthetic.

**2. The "Elevated Casual" Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel** over your **white ribbed tank top** and pair it with your **wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **brown leather belt** to cinch the waist and finish with your **chunky white sneakers**. This look feels much more intentional and chic while keeping the comfort factor high.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a fantastic, versatile addition to your closet! Here are two ways to style that flannel:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** open over the **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and the **black combat boots**. This plays perfectly into that classic streetwear aesthetic.

**2. The Elevated Casual Look**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers** and wear the **oversized flannel shirt** open as a light layer on top. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers** and the **brown leather belt** to tie the earth tones together.
```

```
This flannel is a fantastic find! Since it's oversized, it will pair perfectly with your existing streetwear pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**Outfit 1: The Classic Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized flannel shirt** open over your **white ribbed tank top**. Pair it with your **baggy straight-leg jeans** and **black combat boots**. The fitted tank creates a nice balance against the volume of the flannel and jeans.

**Outfit 2: The Elevated Streetwear Look**
Tuck the **white ribbed tank top** into your **wide-leg khaki trousers** and wear the **oversized flannel shirt** unbuttoned over the top. Add your **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag** for a clean, effortless vibe.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This is a great find! A 90s track jacket is a versatile staple that fits perfectly with your streetwear-leaning wardrobe. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (worn underneath, unzipped)
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the authentic 90s aesthetic. The fitted tank balances the relaxed fit of the jacket and jeans perfectly.

**Option 2: Elevated Contrast**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the sporty track jacket with structured trousers and combat boots creates a cool, "model-off-duty" look that feels intentional and modern.
```


Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s track jacket is a fantastic find—it’s versatile, lightweight, and hits that perfect retro streetwear vibe. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**Look 1: The Off-Duty Streetwear Fit**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (worn unzipped)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* The fitted tank balances the baggy jeans, while the track jacket adds that perfect 90s athletic silhouette.

**Look 2: The Modern Minimalist Mix**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** 90s Track Jacket — Navy/White Stripe (worn zipped up)
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the sporty navy jacket with tailored khaki trousers creates a "high-low" look that feels sophisticated but effortless.
```

```
Finally snagged this vintage Champion track jacket and the fit is honestly perfect for that 90s streetwear vibe. I’m obsessed with how it looks, and it was such a steal at $45 on Poshmark. Can’t wait to rotate this into my daily outfits!
```

```
This 90s track jacket is a fantastic find! Since it's lightweight and hits that perfect streetwear aesthetic, here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The 90s Streetwear Look**
Pair the **90s Track Jacket** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans** and the **White ribbed tank top** underneath (leave the jacket unzipped). Finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** and the **Black crossbody bag** for a classic, effortless vibe.

**2. The Elevated Sporty Look**
Layer the **90s Track Jacket** over the **White ribbed tank top** and pair it with the **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Add the **Chunky white sneakers** to keep the athletic theme going, and use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This track jacket is a great score! It’s versatile enough to lean into both your streetwear and minimal styles. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Classic 90s Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (underneath, unzipped)
*   **Bottom:** Baggy straight-leg jeans
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This leans into the vintage aesthetic while keeping the silhouette balanced between the fitted tank and the baggy denim.

**Option 2: The "Model Off-Duty" Casual Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top
*   **Bottom:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessories:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the sporty navy/white jacket with structured, earthy trousers creates a modern "high-low" look that feels intentional and clean.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This track jacket is a great score! It’s versatile enough to lean into the sporty 90s aesthetic or balance out your more minimal pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Streetwear Uniform**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (underneath, unzipped)
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Black crossbody bag
*   *Why it works:* This plays perfectly into the 90s vintage vibe. The fitted tank balances the baggy silhouette of the jeans and the track jacket.

**Option 2: The Elevated Sporty Look**
*   **Top:** White ribbed tank top (zipped up)
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   **Accessory:** Brown leather belt
*   *Why it works:* Mixing the navy athletic jacket with the khaki trousers creates a clean, "model-off-duty" look that feels intentional and modern.
```

Fit card:

```
Obsessed with this vintage Champion track jacket I just snagged for $45. It’s the perfect sporty layer to throw over everything, and I’m so happy I found it on Poshmark!
```

```
This is a fantastic find! Slip dresses are incredibly versatile because they can easily transition from delicate to edgy. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the slip dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair this with your **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. This balances the femininity of the dress with a cozy, effortless edge.

**2. The "Elevated Casual" Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own and throw your **vintage black denim jacket** over your shoulders. Complete the outfit with your **chunky white sneakers**. The cropped length of the jacket will perfectly complement the midi silhouette of the dress for a balanced, cool-girl aesthetic.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This slip dress is a fantastic find! Because it’s silk and midi-length, it’s incredibly versatile for layering. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The "90s Grunge" Look**
Layer your **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi-hem peek out at the bottom. Pair this with your **black combat boots** for that classic, edgy 90s silhouette. It’s perfect for cooler days while keeping that effortless, vintage vibe.

**2. The "Layered Chic" Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own and throw your **vintage black denim jacket** on top to dress it down. Finish the look with your **chunky white sneakers**. The contrast between the delicate silk and the structured denim keeps the outfit balanced and comfortable for everyday wear.
```


Outfit suggestion:

```
This 90s silk slip dress is a fantastic find—it’s versatile enough to transition from a delicate piece to an edgy streetwear staple. Here are two ways to style it using your current wardrobe:

**1. The 90s Grunge Look**
Layer the **oversized grey crewneck sweatshirt** over the dress, letting the midi hem peek out at the bottom. Pair this with the **black combat boots** and the **black crossbody bag**. The contrast between the soft silk and the slouchy, oversized sweatshirt is quintessential 90s style.

**2. The Edgy Streetwear Look**
Wear the slip dress on its own and throw the **vintage black denim jacket** over your shoulders. Finish the look with the **chunky white sneakers** and the **black crossbody bag**. This creates a perfect balance between the feminine floral print and the rugged, vintage denim.
```

```

**2. The "Elevated Casual" Look**

```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This baby tee is a fantastic find! It balances your closet perfectly by adding a pop of color and a feminine touch to your more structured pieces. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Y2K Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** layered on top (left unzipped) and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers** and your **Black crossbody bag**.

**Option 2: Trendy "Soft-Minimalist" Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots** for a cool, vintage-inspired edge.
```

- selected_item: Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print ($18.0, depop)
- search_results: 2

Outfit suggestion:

```
This baby tee is a fantastic find! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will balance out your wider bottoms perfectly. Here are two ways to style it:

**Look 1: The Y2K Streetwear Vibe**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Finish the look with your **Black cropped zip hoodie** (worn open or layered over) and your **Chunky white sneakers**. The contrast between the tight tee and baggy jeans is a classic Y2K silhouette.

**Look 2: Effortless Cottagecore/Minimalist**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use the **Brown leather belt** to define your waist, and pair it with your **Black combat boots** to give the soft butterfly print a bit of an edgy, grunge-inspired finish.
```


```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! It’s versatile enough to lean into your streetwear roots or a more relaxed aesthetic. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The Y2K Streetwear Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash
*   **Outerwear:** Black cropped zip hoodie
*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers
*   *Why it works:* The fitted tee balances the volume of your baggy jeans, while the cropped hoodie adds a modern, sporty layer.

**Option 2: The Soft Minimalist Look**
*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print
*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers
*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket
*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots
*   *Why it works:* The butterfly print adds a feminine touch to the structured khaki trousers, and the denim jacket provides a cohesive, vintage edge.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe—it adds a playful touch to your existing staples! Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: The "Y2K Streetwear" Look**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Black cropped zip hoodie** on top (worn unzipped) and finish with your **Chunky white sneakers** for a classic, effortless vintage vibe.

**Option 2: The "Soft-Minimalist" Look**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Use your **Brown leather belt** to cinch the waist, and pair with the **Black combat boots** to give the soft butterfly graphic a bit of an edgy, intentional contrast.
```

Outfit suggestion:

```
This Y2K baby tee is a perfect addition to your wardrobe! Because it’s fitted and cropped, it will create a great silhouette when balanced with your roomier bottoms. Here are two ways to style it:

**Option 1: Effortless Streetwear**
Pair the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** with your **Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash**. Add the **Vintage black denim jacket** on top for a layered look and finish with the **Chunky white sneakers**. The contrast between the tight tee and baggy denim is quintessential Y2K.

**Option 2: Soft Minimalist**
Tuck the **Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print** into your **Wide-leg khaki trousers**. Cinch the waist with the **Brown leather belt** to pull the earth tones together, and complete the outfit with your **Black combat boots** for a bit of edge.
```

